                                                                           2026年中期報告 2026 INTERIM REPORT          19
管理層討論及分析
Management Discussion and Analysis

回顧與展望                                     REVIEW AND PROSPECTS

2026年上半年，國內房地產與消費市場呈現                     In the first half of 2026, the domestic real estate and consumer markets
差異化發展態勢。住宅地產層面，政策重心從                      exhibited differentiated development trends. In terms of residential real
「控風險」向「穩預期、促修復」深度演進，核心                    estate, the focus of policies shifted significantly from “controlling risks”
邏輯圍繞城市更新、住房消費與資產屬性重塑                      to “stabilising expectations and promoting recovery”, with the core logic
三大支柱展開，行業整體呈現「量縮價穩、結                      centering on three key pillars: urban renewal, housing consumption, and
構分化」的顯著特徵，核心城市率先顯現企穩                      reshaping of asset attributes. The industry in general showed significant
跡象，而全國層面仍處於深度調整後的磨底階                      characteristics of “trading volume contracts as prices stabilize, with

或然負債                                      Contingent Liabilities

貸款提供擔保。本集團的擔保期通常自授出按                      guarantee its purchasers’ mortgage loans. The Group’s guarantee period
揭日期開始。預售住宅物業按揭擔保一般於                       typically starts from the date of the grant of the mortgage. Guarantees
下列最早者解除：(i)政府機關向買家發放房地                    for mortgages on pre-sold residential properties are generally discharged
產所有權證時；或(ii)買家結清物業的按揭貸款                   at the earlier of: (i) the issue of the real estate ownership certificate
時。本集團已就授予本集團物業購買者的按揭                      by government authorities to the purchasers; or (ii) the satisfaction of
貸款融資向金融機構提供擔保。                            mortgage loans by the purchasers of the properties. The Group has
                                          provided guarantees to financial institutions for mortgage loan facilities
                                          granted to purchasers of the Group’s properties.

股份獎勵計劃                                    SHARE AWARD SCHEME

於2019年11月1日，董事會決議採納股份獎勵                   On 1 November 2019, the Board has resolved to adopt a share award
計劃（「股份獎勵計劃」）。股份獎勵計劃的宗旨                    scheme (the “Share Award Scheme”). The objectives of the Share
為：(i)肯定本公司僱員（「僱員」）所作出的貢獻                  Award Scheme are: (i) to recognize the contribution of employees of the
及獎勵僱員對本集團未來的長期發展繼續作出                      Company (the “Employees”) and to provide incentives for the Employees
重大貢獻；及(ii)吸引及挽留可能對本集團成長                   to continuously make substantial contributions for the Group’s long-term
及發展有益的優秀僱員。                               growth in the future; and (ii) to attract and retain talented Employees who
                                          may be beneficial to the growth and development of the Group.

 董事會就管理股份獎勵計劃而委任的受託人                      The aggregated maximum number of shares of the Company (the
（「受託人」）   （為獨立第三方）可購買的最高本公                “Shares”) that the trustee (who is an independent third party) appointed
 司股份（「股份」）數目累計不得超過本公司於                    by the Board to manage the Share Award Scheme (the “Trustee”) may
 2019年11月1日全部已發行股本的0.5%，即                 purchase must not exceed 0.5% of the total share capital in issue of the
 29,495,000股股份（佔本報告日期本公司已發                Company on 1 November 2019, i.e. 29,495,000 shares (representing
 行股份約0.41%）  。                            approximately 0.41% of the issued shares of the Company as at the date
                                          of this report).

效期為自2019年11月1日採納日期起至2029年                 effective for ten years from the adoption date of 1 November 2019 and
10月31日止十年。於本報告日期，股份獎勵計                    up to 31 October 2029. As at the date of this report, the remaining life of
劃的剩餘年期約為三年又兩個月。                           the Share Award Scheme was around three years and two months.

    5.4 資本風險管理                                          5.4 Capital risk management

         本集團的資本管理目標為保障本集團                                  The Group’s objectives when managing capital are to safeguard
         能繼續營運，以為股東提供回報和維                                  the Group’s ability to continue as a going concern in order to
         持最佳的資本結構以降低資金成本。                                  provide returns for shareholders and to maintain an optimal
                                                           capital structure to reduce the cost of capital.

         為維持或調整資本結構，本集團可能                                  In order to maintain or adjust the capital structure, the Group
         會調整支付予股東的股息金額、發行                                  may adjust the amount of dividends paid to shareholders, issue
         新股份或出售資產以減少債務。                                    new shares or sell assets to reduce debt.

         本集團利用資產負債比率監察其資                                   The Group monitors capital on the basis of the gearing ratio.
         本。此比率按照債務淨額除以資本總                                  This ratio is calculated as net debt divided by total capital. Net
         額計算。債務淨額按借款總額加租賃                                  debt is calculated as total borrowings plus lease liabilities less
         負債減現金及現金等價物計算。資本                                  cash and cash equivalents. Total capital is calculated as total
         總額按合併財務狀況表所列示的權益                                  equity, as shown in the consolidated statement of financial
         總額加上債務淨額計算。                                       position, plus net debt.

    元（截至2025年6月30日止六個月：人民                   months ended 30 June 2025: RMB14,935,000) with capitalisation
   幣14,935,000元），資本化年利率為5.97%               rate of 5.97% (Six months ended 30 June 2025: 5.80%) per annum.
   （截至2025年6月30日止六個月：5.80%）。

    於 2026 年 6 月 30 日 ， 賬 面 值 為 人 民 幣       Investment properties with a carrying amount of
    112,289,641,000元（2025年12月31日：人          RMB112,289,641,000 as at 30 June 2026 (31 December 2025:

    本集團所有投資物業的公允價值均以第三                      Fair value of all of the Group’s investment properties are measured at
    級計量，有重大不可觀察輸入值。                         Level 3, with significant unobservable inputs.

    期內第一、二及三級之間並無轉撥。                        There were no transfers between Level 1, 2 and 3 during the period.

    所有投資物業於2026年6月30日及2025年                 All investment properties were revalued as at 30 June 2026 and 31
    12月31日由獨立專業合資格估值師戴德                     December 2025 by Cushman & Wakefield International Property
    梁行國際物業顧問（Cushman & Wakefield            Advisers, the independent professional qualified valuer.
    International Property Advisers）重估。

    本集團就將予開發及在建投資物業分別採                      For investment properties to be developed and under development,
    用直接比較法及投資法。直接比較法乃假                      the Group adopted direct comparison approach and investment
    設該等物業各自按現況交吉出售。經參考                      approach. Direct comparison approach is assuming sale of each
    有關市場的銷售交易，選擇鄰近地區的可                      of these properties in its existing state with the benefit of vacant
    資比較物業並就位置及物業規模等因素的                      possession. By making reference to sales transactions as available
    差異作出調整。                                 in the relevant market, comparable properties in close proximity have
                                            been selected and adjustments have been made to account for the
                                            difference in factors such as locations and property size.

    投資法計及已產生建設成本及完成該開發                      Investment approach has taken into account the construction
    項目將產生的成本以反映已竣工開發項目                      costs incurred and the costs that will be incurred to complete the
    的質量，依據是該等物業將根據本集團最                      development to reflect the quality of the completed development
    近開發計劃開發和竣工。就已竣工投資物                      on the basis that the properties will be developed and completed
    業而言，估值根據投資法透過將來自現有                      in accordance with the Group’s latest development plan. For
    租約的淨租金收入（經適當計及物業復歸潛                     completed investment properties, valuations were based on
    在收入）資本化，並經參考於活躍市場上的                     investment approach by capitalisation of the net rental income
    可資比較價格後作出。                              derived from the existing tenancy agreements with due allowance for
                                            reversionary income potential of the properties and by reference to
                                            comparable price in an active market.

    期內估值方法並無變動。                             There were no changes to the valuation techniques during the
                                            period.

23.所得稅開支
       （續）                                23. INCOME TAX EXPENSE (continued)

    開曼群島所得稅                                 Cayman Island income tax

    本公司為根據開曼群島公司法於開曼群島                      The Company is incorporated in the Cayman Islands as an exempted
    註冊成立的獲豁免有限公司，因此，獲豁                      company with limited liability under the Companies Act of Cayman
    免繳納開曼群島所得稅。                             Islands and accordingly, is exempted from Cayman Islands income
                                            tax.

    英屬處女群島所得稅                               BVI income tax

    根據英屬處女群島現行法律，本集團於英                      Under the current laws of BVI, the Group’s subsidiaries incorporated
